Bug found and fixed: session fallback was stripping ALL optional columns when only some were missing.
- **Lesson-duration guard tightened** (codex-20260622-lesson-wrap): one planned wrap-window rule for AGI and AudioSocket. Continues teaching before planned 6-min wrap (`TARGET_WRAP_SECONDS=360`) even if model emits closing phrases after 5-min minimum. Callers can end immediately with goodbye. Hard 8-min cap (`MAX_CALL_SECONDS=480`). Commit `03a6d86`.
- **Wrong-answer bump-down scaffolding made skill-specific** (codex-20260622-scaffold-ladders): 3-level repair ladders for counting, addition, subtraction, multiplication, division, word problems, oral literacy. Live prompts inject `REQUIRED BUMP-DOWN LADDER` with teacher move, easier question, rebuild move, avoid rule. ### July 7 — Intron async key + STT deep research (session 400bc972)
Naomi supplied a new Intron key that works for **async STT API** (`POST /file/v1/upload` + `GET /file/v1/status/{file_id}`) but still 403s on sync (`/file/v1/upload/sync`). `sabi-server/stt.py` now keeps sync first, then falls back to Intron async upload/status polling when sync denied. Also pads very short WAVs to 1.2s before Intron upload, handles 429 `Retry-After`. `/admin/stt/intron-test` proof: numeracy clip `addition_total_spend_15_30` returned `provider="intron"`, transcript `45`, `intron_status="FILE_TRANSCRIBED"`, latency ~2.9s. Short literacy clip `lit_blend_cat` reached Intron after padding but transcribed as `Khart`, proving Intron reachable but not production-promotable without offline gold-set evaluation.

**Deep STT research** (400bc972) — AfriSpeech-MultiBench (Nov 2025) NG WER: Intron-Sahara V2 **12.83%**, Azure 26.41, Gemini-2.0 Flash 26.47, Whisper-large-v3 **26.53**, Google Chirp V3 27.10, AWS Transcribe 28.16, GPT-4o Transcribe **40.03**. ⚠️ Heavy caveat: paper is Intron-authored, Intron datasets, 16kHz not 8kHz telephony, adults not children. Trust direction, not decimal. Nollywood ASR paper (arXiv 2407.02631): Whisper **124% WER** on Nigerian film audio.

**Number-biasing shipped** (commit `3358dae`, Jul 7): when numeric answer expected (via `has_numeric_lesson_context`), local Whisper gets number-word `hotwords` + NO prompt (prompt makes short clips echo it back); Groq gets `local_numeric_salvage` that retries local+hotwords only when a number was expected but Groq didn't hear one. Recovers "thirty"/"fifteen" that Groq renders "Thank you"/"Shusin", never forces a number onto a name/sentence. 14/14 regression. **KEYPAD DTMF deliberately NOT used for answers** (Naomi: "a child pressing a key defeats the spoken-answer assessment"; keypad only as post-repeated-failure fallback).

**Intron "noise robustness" claim REFUTED**: its "0% false triggers on silence" tests hallucination-on-silence, not background noise. On actual noisy Parliamentary test: Distil-Whisper-v3.5 (11.5%) BEAT Intron-Sahara V2 (12.01%).

**VAD guard SHIPPED to prod** (Jul 7, commit `aeeb0a3`): Silero VAD pre-gate at top of `SpeechToText.transcribe()`. No speech above threshold → return empty. Settings onset=0.5, min_silence 500ms, speech_pad 200ms; fail-open; toggle `SABI_STT_VAD_GATE=0`. Validated e2e on 20 clips: gates 3 dead-air turns, keeps all 17 real answers. On 2 turns where groq/lv3/distil all hallucinated ("Subtitles by the Amara.org community"), VAD stack returned EMPTY.

**Awarri NG-accent model tested — WORSE on noise**. Whisper-Small + no VAD + trained on adult formal NG English → on noisy child turns it hallucinates fluent adult sentences ("the federal government has ordered ongoing legal proceedings", "how can we reduce the stigma around seeking mental health support"). Clean turns fine/more-Nigerian ("mummy" not "mommy"). Implication: **Sabi's problem is NOISE+children+telephony, not accent** — accent-tuning alone doesn't fix it.

### July 7 — feedback-timing fix + Cerebras/Anthropic
Root-caused **why testers hang up before feedback**: `voice_realtime._should_offer_feedback` returns `not hungup and end_reason in FEEDBACK_OFFER_END_REASONS` — so end-of-call feedback is ONLY offered on graceful Sabi wrap while line is still open. Natural exit (caller presses red button) sets `hungup=True` → feedback never asked. Verified on last 2 real calls: `be48a49b` hung up at 3:08 with `ended_before_minimum_lesson_window` → NO feedback sidecar; `894b6040` natural wrap 8:47 → feedback captured BUT the prompt itself played **23.5s** before the beep.

**Built the frustration hotkey** (Naomi's ask): caller presses **`*` anytime** → Sabi stops mid-sentence and opens the open feedback space, instead of hanging up. All in `voice_realtime.py`: new `FEEDBACK_HOTKEY_DIGIT` (`*`, env `SABI_FEEDBACK_HOTKEY_DIGIT`), `_consume_feedback_hotkey()` (latches hotkey, re-queues other DTMF so keypad fallback survives), detection wired into `play_pcm_with_barge` + `wait_for_utterance`, loop breaks with `end_reason="feedback_hotkey"`, `record_feedback_note(hotkey=True)` uses warm `FEEDBACK_HOTKEY_PROMPT_TEXT`, records EVEN when `FEEDBACK_MODE` is restricted (explicit press overrides), tags sidecar `hotkey`. Greeting appends `FEEDBACK_HOTKEY_ANNOUNCE_TEXT` ("press the star key anytime and I will stop and listen"). End-of-call + requested prompts reworded warmer, openly invite complaints. New `feedback_hotkey_regression.py` 10/10; `feedback_capture_regression.py` 31/31.

**What Cerebras actually is**: fast LLM inference provider (~2100 tok/s custom chips), configured as Sabi's first fast-path LLM (Llama-3.3-70b) ahead of Claude Haiku→Groq→Ollama. Live reality Jul 7: `CEREBRAS_API_KEY` NOT in Hetzner `.env`, log says `LLM primary: Claude claude-haiku-4-5`. Cerebras is dormant on prod — no wasted round-trip.

**Deployed Jul 8 ~02:06** (backups `voice_realtime.py.backup-fbflow-20260708-020554`, `main.py.backup-fbflow-20260708-020554`). Container regressions: hotkey 21, feedback 31, flash 14, phone_config 15, intron_isolation 18. `/asterisk/inbound-route` returns `flash` for +234/080/US numbers → every caller on flash-callback, nobody charged. `FLASH_CALLBACK_ALL=True` in container.

**Hotkey upgraded into a mid-call feedback CONVERSATION**: trigger = press `*` OR say anything feedback-ish (`_looks_like_feedback_request`). Sabi stops, records+transcribes+saves note, then **`handle_mid_call_feedback()`**: LLM generates ONE warm empathy sentence (guardrailed, fallback fixed caring line), Sabi speaks it + a deterministic "Do you want to keep learning, or should we stop here for today?", listens, `_wants_to_continue_lesson()` decides → **resume from where they left off** or **stop** (`end_reason=feedback_then_stopped`). `record_feedback_note` returns transcript (`-&gt; str`) and sets `self.feedback_captured`; end-of-call offer skipped if a note was already captured mid-call. Notes tagged `mid_call`/`hotkey`. Greeting rewording per Naomi: *"Anytime you want, you can say, I want to leave feedback, or press the star key on your phone, and I will stop and listen after the beep."*

**Flash-callback-for-everyone**: `main.py` new `SABI_FLASH_CALLBACK_ALL` (default `1`) → `/asterisk/inbound-route` returns `flash` for EVERY valid caller. Reverses Jul 6 conditional-test route. Dialplan already branches on this endpoint, no Asterisk reload needed. Cost implication: we pay every outbound callback. Set flag `0` to revert.

**Anthropic minor-serving request = NOT sent** — Naomi must send from E4E Anthropic Console.

Because the earlier login bug was NOT a crash — the app just showed "wrong password" — monitoring had to include *handled* user-facing errors. Plan doc: `curriculum-app/docs/SABI_NEVER_MISS_AN_ERROR_PLAN.md`.

- **Phase 1 LIVE on prod**: `lib/monitoring/report.ts` `reportUserFacingError(flow, msg, extra)` → Sentry `captureMessage` tagged `user_facing:true` + `flow`. Wired into sign-in (password / magic-link / Google). `lib/monitoring/alert.ts` `sendAlert(title, body, severity)` → **dual channel: WhatsApp (Twilio) + email (Resend)**. `app/api/monitoring/daily-digest/route.ts` + `vercel.json` cron `0 13 * * *` (Vercel Hobby = once/day max). **Verified end-to-end Jul 23: email delivered, WhatsApp delivered + read.**
- Keys: `MONITORING_SENTRY_TOKEN` (Naomi's personal token with issues:read + alerts:write + project:write); Sentry org `sabi-43`, projects `javascript-nextjs` (website/sign-in) + `sabi-server` (phone pipeline). Twilio WhatsApp sandbox `whatsapp:+14155238886` → Naomi's `+18604367048`.
- **Phase 2 LIVE (cloud monitor + robot user)**: `.github/workflows/monitor.yml` + `.github/monitor/monitor.mjs` — GitHub Actions every 5 min, independent of Vercel. Uptime, new user-facing Sentry issues (`age:-10m`), robot user. State in `state.json` cached between runs via `actions/cache` (no spam). `app/api/monitoring/robot`: real signup→confirm→login→cleanup against prod Supabase each run. 11 GitHub Actions secrets set via `gh secret set`.
- **Phase 3 LIVE (dead-man's switch)**: Sentry **cron monitor `sabi-cloud-monitor`** (schedule `*/5`, margin 15). monitor.mjs checks in every run (`ok`/`error`). Vercel daily digest independently verifies cloud monitor still checking in, leads with "The safety monitor STOPPED — I am flying blind" if not. Two independent watchers.

Gotchas learned: (a) **Sentry cron check-ins MUST use the DSN ingest URL** — the web API `/monitors/&lt;slug&gt;/checkins/` returns 405; (b) `vercel env pull` returns EMPTY values for sensitive vars (earlier "EMERGENCY_ALERT_PHONE empty" conclusion was probably WRONG); (c) repo/branch trap: work was being committed to `feature/advisor-board-notion` while `git push origin main` pushed stale main — Vercel deploys still worked because `vercel --prod` uploads working tree.

**Task sign-off** shipped same day: Notion Tasks DB got `Signed Off By` (rich_text) + `Signed Off At` (date) via API. `POST /api/notion/tasks/[id]/sign-off` guarded by `isBoardMemberUser` stamps signed-in member's display name. Green "Sign off ✓" button becomes "✍️ Signed by X · date". **One-click from email** (no login) via HMAC-signed 30-day tokens (secret = `[REDACTED:sensitive-label]` || `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`). `GET/POST /api/tasks/sign-off` public. Assignment email leads with green "✅ Sign off" button carrying per-recipient token.

### August 7-8 — Sabi rebuilt on Gemini Live (session 019fdd47, memory 1f5e4922)
This is the biggest architectural pivot of the summer. Twilio **+1 (715) 312-2345** → SIP trunk → Asterisk `from-twilio` → `sabi-inbound` → AudioSocket **9020**. Old ElevenLabs managed-agent URL still in Twilio config but bypassed.

**Gemini Live canary**: Naomi's number `+1 860-436-7048` alone runs one persistent `gemini-3.1-flash-live-preview` session (voice initially `Kore`) that owns listening, reasoning, speech, VAD, and native barge-in — **no separate STT step, no Claude Haiku, no ElevenLabs TTS on that lane**. Everyone else stays on the established pipeline. Scoped via `SABI_GEMINI_LIVE_PHONES` / `phone_uses_gemini_live()`. Her number is also forced numeracy-only. Code: `sabi-server/gemini_live.py`, doc `sabi-server/docs/GEMINI_LIVE_PHONE_CANARY.md`.

**Self-hosted turn-taking** built before the Live switch (still used on non-Live lanes): Silero speech gate + echo comparator vs Sabi's outgoing audio + **Pipecat Smart Turn v3** endpointing in `sabi-server/turn_taking.py`. Replaced a 2-frame/RMS-420 energy trigger that turned coughs and echo into turns. Docs: `SELF_HOSTED_TURN_TAKING.md`, `ELEVENLABS_CLEANROOM_PARITY.md`, `OPENAI_REALTIME_BARGE_IN_BLUEPRINT.md`.

**Grading is deterministic, not Gemini's opinion**: numeric-first ("thirty fries" = 30), a registered-question tool so Gemini cannot ask a problem the backend isn't grading, an enforced 5-7 minute lesson clock, an "I don't know → teach one step" gate, and a beginner deck that starts 2×2 → 2×3 with larger facts locked. **Tutor prompt shrank 32,555 → ~3.7k chars.** Mastery rule from research: 4 of the last 5 **independent** probes across ≥2 calls; unclear audio is "not scorable", never wrong. See `docs/FLN_GRADING_RESEARCH_AND_SABI_STANDARD_2026-08-07.md`.

**Known limitation**: Gemini Live's visible `inputAudioTranscription` often mis-renders Nigerian 8 kHz phone speech — "thirty" → "Tati", foreign-language text — while the model's *semantic* hearing is frequently still right. Replay tooling: `scripts/replay_gemini_live_hearing.py`, `scripts/probe_gemini_live_clips.py`, `scripts/replay_gemini_live_archive.py`.

**Operational rule learned the hard way**: recreating the sabi-server container kills any live AudioSocket call (cut a 371s call on Aug 7). Always check `docker exec sabi-asterisk asterisk -rx "core show channels concise"` before `docker compose up -d sabi`.

### August 10-11 — numeric STT sidecar (session 1f5e4922)
On every finished **numeric** turn of a Gemini Live call, Groq `whisper-large-v3` and local faster-whisper re-transcribe the same canonical 16 kHz clip and their votes are recorded. **Shadow only** — cannot change Sabi's reply, grade or timing. `SABI_NUMERIC_SIDECAR_MODE` accepts only `shadow`. Rules: votes never see question/operands/expected answer; both engines get byte-identical audio (SHA-256 recorded); only agreement on single numeric value counts; object nouns ignored ("two mangoes" = "two fries" = 2); teen/tens conflicts (13/30) abstain; late/dropped/crashed votes recorded but never applied. Files: `sabi-server/numeric_sidecar.py`, vote engines + `_canonical_numeric_clip` in `stt.py`, attachment `gemini_live.py: GeminiLiveCallRunner._submit_numeric_sidecar`, 39 offline checks in `numeric_sidecar_regression.py`. Commit `96710a6`. Records at `/shared/audio/numeric_sidecar/&lt;call_uuid&gt;.jsonl`. Read via `scripts/numeric_sidecar_report.py`.

**Second vote is Azure Speech as of Aug 11** (commit `d26a60f`). Gold-clip bake-off on 7 labelled Nigerian phone clips: Groq `whisper-large-v3` 0/7 median 0.25s; local faster-whisper + hotwords 2/7 median 11s; Gemini Live (raw + contextual) 2/7; **Azure Speech 4/7 median 0.71s**. Azure cracked `thirty-turn-05` (every Whisper renders "Tati") and is the only engine to ever recover "Her name is Gideon". Caveats: **`en-NG` and `en-US` returned byte-identical output on all 7 clips** — win is Azure's model, not the Nigerian locale. Azure resource `sabi-speech-test`, RG `sabi-stt`, **East US**, Free F0, on her Azure for Students subscription. ⚠️ Wesleyan tenant — same migration trap as Google billing account; move before production. Key at `secrets/AZURE_SPEECH_KEY` + `AZURE_SPEECH_REGION`. `SABI_NUMERIC_SIDECAR_SECOND_ENGINE=auto|azure|local_whisper`.

**Aug 12: drop Intron and ElevenLabs Scribe**. Both blocked on account permissions (Intron 403, ElevenLabs 401 "missing speech_to_text"). Two votes are Groq + Azure only.

### August 20 — the big Live-lane-to-production sprint (multiple sessions)
Naomi's framing: *"i had seperate lane because we were working on it - but had every intention to port it to the new lane."* Her order at 19:34 UTC: **"leave literacy for now do 1-4."**

Four steps:
1. **Port `guard_input` + `raise_safeguarding_incident` into `gemini_live.py`** — non-negotiable with children. ⚠️ `guard_output` cannot be ported as-is: on turn-based lane you filter model's text before a word is spoken; on native audio, speech streams as generated, so by the time you have transcript, child has already heard it. Nearest equivalent is what early-wrap guard does — detect on streaming transcript and drop *unplayed* remainder from queue.
2. Literacy — deferred on Naomi's instruction (Live lane has no literacy: 19 multiplication problems, numeracy tools, "numeracy tutor" prompt).
3. Flip `phone_uses_gemini_live()` from opt-in allowlist → opt-out, keeping per-number escape hatch.
4. Measure cost for a week — native audio bills differently than Haiku+Groq chain, nobody knows the number.

**Feedback capture wired into Live lane**: caller says "I want to leave feedback" / complain / report a problem / leave a note → Sabi goes silent, **same beep**, next turn stored as note (not graded, does not touch learning state), lands in `/admin/feedback` in identical format. Cues: `FEEDBACK_OPEN_CUE`, `FEEDBACK_SAVED_CUE`, `SAFEGUARDING_CUE`. 20 regressions passed. The `*` star key was never broken on the Live lane — **it was never there**. Star key + spoken trigger live in `voice_realtime.py:245`; `gemini_live.py` had zero feedback references. Any star-key test from Naomi's own number was testing a lane where feature did not exist.

**In flight and UNCOMMITTED in `sabi-server/` as of 15:37 ET**: `gemini_live.py` (+231 lines), `call_admin.py`, `phone_utils.py`, `gemini_live_regression.py`, untracked `scripts/gemini_live_cost_report.py`. Last commit on main is `eccabf0`.

⚠️ **The whole `pilot/` tree is in NO git repo** (only `curriculum-app/` and `sabi-server/` are repos). Every testing doc, DPIA, pre-pilot plans exist on this laptop only — against Naomi's own GitHub-first rule, and `DPIA_SABI.md` is cited by 15 other docs.

**Safety gap verified in code Aug 20**: `gemini_live.py` imports **only** `SABI_SAFETY_PREAMBLE` from `guardrails.py`. Deterministic pattern layer — `guard_input`, `guard_output`, `raise_safeguarding_incident` — called **only from `llm.py`** (turn-based lane). So on Gemini Live a crisis disclosure is handled by prompt-following alone: **no regex crisis detection, no output filtering, no safeguarding incident ever raised.** Matters before any child calls that lane.

**Prompt now in a file, inline fallback is stale**: `prompts/sabi_tutor_prompt.md` (74 lines) is live prompt, loaded by `_load_tutor_prompt()`; `_FALLBACK_TUTOR_PROMPT` string inside `gemini_live.py` only used if file missing — and it is now **out of date** (still carries "Yeees!/Ehn ehn/Sharp sharp" which commit `ca6dedf` removed from live file in favour of "Be warm and familiar.").

**Lesson clock + deck**: `SABI_MAX_CALL_SECONDS=480` hard deadline; min lesson 300s; target wrap 420s. Deck = 19 `MULTIPLICATION_PROBLEMS` in 3 difficulty tiers, answers 3→48; tier set by saved `multiplication_difficulty_tier`, never inferred from mastery. Audio in 8 kHz, Gemini out 24 kHz downsampled by `Pcm24kTo8k`. Three Gemini tools: `get_next_numeracy_problem`, `grade_numeric_answer`, `get_lesson_progress` (must be called before any wrap-up). Anti-drift: `is_early_wrap_text` blocks goodbye &lt;300s (max 3 repairs); `registered_followup_enforcement` re-asks reserved question if Gemini invents its own.

**The deck is what made her sound scripted — tools OFF on the live lane Aug 20 (session 69b43c76)**. Naomi's verdict once tools removed: *"sounds much closer to original Sabi."* `SABI_GEMINI_LIVE_TOOLS` now three-way: `on` = deck-driven (old), `off` = **live setting**, no `functionDeclarations` sent at all, `ledger` = written but never deployed. In `off` mode Sabi invents her own naira questions and marks them herself, exactly like Claude Haiku system. Why deck had to go — call `715af326` at 16:15: tool registered `eqg_a_2x2_eggs` ("Two plates have two eggs each", expected 4), Sabi ignored it and invented *"You buy groundnuts for 20 Naira, and biscuits for 10 Naira. How much are you going to pay altogether?"* Naomi answered **30 — correct** — and `grade_numeric_answer` scored 30 against 4 and returned `incorrect`. Sabi abandoned the naira question. Root cause: `registered_followup_from_tool_events` only enforces a problem registered by a *correct grade*, so opening question of every call was never enforced.

**Three separate brevity caps, not one**: removing "keep it short" from prompt doesn't work, because it was written in three places — prose block, `IMPORTANT RULES` line (both in `prompts/sabi_tutor_prompt.md`), AND "Speak warmly in one or two short sentences" inside `build_live_call_constraints` in `gemini_live.py` (injected per call, invisible from markdown). All three removed. Naomi's standing preference: **brevity is the wrong target, the longer register is what stops Sabi feeling transactional.**

**Numeric sidecar has never once influenced a call**: pure shadow by construction. `submit()` queues to thread pool and returns bool, nothing awaits it, `sidecar.drain` runs only *after* `memory.clear_call` at teardown. Measured on `715af326`: deadline 1.5s vs actual 3.24/3.59/3.97s, `decision_eligible: 0`, every turn `stale=True`. Also gates on `expected_answer is None`, so with tools `off` it is **completely inert**. Naomi's idea worth building: use two re-hearers as *hearing consensus only* (no answer key needed) and let Sabi self-correct one turn later — 3.5s lands before next turn even though misses same-turn deadline. ⚠️ Groq key returned **403** Aug 20, that lane may be dead.

**Restart trap Aug 20**: running container had `SABI_GEMINI_LIVE_VOICE=auto` as runtime override while `.env` on disk said `Leda`. Recreating container reverted live voice to Leda. Fixed by writing `auto` into `.env` durably. Lesson: before running `safe_deploy.sh`, diff what container reports against what `.env` says.

**Carrier switched back to Africa's Talking Aug 20** — `SABI_SIP_PROVIDER=africastalking`, `SABI_CALLER_ID=+2342017001459`. Driven by cost, not audio: Twilio Nigeria termination $0.2263/min, account is Trial that only dials one verified number. Rollback = set provider `twilio` + caller ID `+17153122345`. ⚠️ **The reason she left AT — Sabi could not hear her — is unfixed.** Cost and audio are separate problems; this switch only addressed cost.

**"Text CALL and Sabi rings you"** built Aug 20 (session e254ca93): `curriculum-app/lib/voice/call-request.ts` `requestSabiCall()` POSTs to voice server's `/admin/asterisk/direct-call` with `X-API-Key`. Channel-agnostic. Two webhooks call it: `app/api/sabi/sms/incoming` (AT) and `app/api/sabi/whatsapp/incoming` (Twilio). Intent classification + keyword stripping in `lib/voice/inbound-intent.ts`. Keywords: CALL / CALL ME / RING / LESSON / TEACH. Guards fail CLOSED — `SABI_CALL_REQUEST_ALLOWLIST` unset authorises nobody; cooldown 180s; daily cap 8. Counted from `sabi_sms_log` rows of type `call_request_dispatched`, NOT process memory (serverless doesn't share state). Vercel prod env set: `SABI_SERVER_URL=https://api.eduforequality.org`, `SABI_CALL_REQUEST_ALLOWLIST=+18604367048`. **PROVEN end to end Aug 20**: simulated inbound WhatsApp → call_request → dispatch → `PJSIP/africastalking-00000030 is ringing`. Twilio config side: fixed WhatsApp sandbox sender's inbound webhook (was Twilio stock demo `timberwolf-mastiff-9776.twil.io/demo-reply`), fixed `+17153122345` `sms_url` missing `?key=`.

**5-adult tester round plan** (session e254ca93): Five testers, five days, minimum one call a day; **Sabi calls them, they pay nothing.** Day 1 baseline → Day 2 memory + shared-phone "no, this is someone else" test → Day 3 noise → Day 4 number-stress list → Day 5 break her. And the round-damaging one: **"she will mishear numbers — don't report it."** Backwards — mishearing is exactly what testers must report, in detail. **Feedback strategy settled Aug 20**: WhatsApp voice note right *for how it felt*, wrong for misheard numbers (ask one typed line each: *"I said thirty, she heard thirteen"*). Real objection was organisation — `/admin/feedback` already is cloud-organized, note glued to exact `call_uuid` and second, audio + transcript, auto-redacted; `SABI_ADMIN_PIN` gives read-only `/admin/*` so **Sonia can triage without holding write key**.

**Voice hunting ended in one finding**: Gemini has **no Nigerian voices** — 30 prebuilts, no accent/locale variants, no `en-NG` in Gemini TTS or Cloud TTS (only `en-ZA`), and no API lists them. Native audio **adapts to the voice it hears**: fed Naomi's real voice memo, both Kore and Leda answered in Pidgin ("How you dey", "Ah, nne!") — text probes never did that. Sending **no voice at all** (`auto`) is what Naomi called "so so much better", and with no voice pinned Gemini need not pick same one twice. Rolled back to Kore ~17:16 ET: *"this sounds like a lot - please roll back to the kore from earlier today - and we can pick this up another time."* Voice only — nothing else reverted.

⚠️ **Commit `6cbe0ea "promote Gemini Live to the production lane"` is committed and pushed but NOT deployed** as of Aug 20. Running container and `/opt/sabi/sabi-server` both lack it (`grep -c SAFEGUARDING_CUE = 0`). `phone_uses_gemini_live` on box is still opt-IN allowlist version. `safe_deploy.sh` only runs `docker compose up -d --no-deps sabi` against existing image, so plain redeploy WON'T ship it — but **any `docker compose build` or file copy will**, and that flips Gemini Live on for every caller.

### August 20-21 — Africa's Talking really does route to us (session 1b5d89b4)
Verified myself on the box: `docker logs sabi-asterisk` shows `from-at` context firing 4 times today on `PJSIP/africastalking-*` channels, all dialled to `+2342017001459`. Earlier note claiming "no SIP traffic from AT" is FALSE — delete it. Why missed: `/etc/asterisk/cdr.conf` leaves `unanswered = no`, and every AT leg so far was flash ring-and-hangup or unanswered outbound — none of which writes a CDR row. All 41 CDR rows are Twilio because Twilio legs are only ones ever *answered*. **Read Asterisk log, not CDR, for AT.**

**Real failure — callback went out on wrong carrier**: each AT flash triggered `Called +2349044218460@twilio` / `Called +2348124724459@twilio`. Twilio is Trial account with one verified caller ID (`+18604367048`), so cannot dial Nigerian number at all. Those three callers rang in, hung up, never called back.

Config now right (`.env` + container both rewritten 21:16 UTC): `SABI_SIP_PROVIDER=africastalking`, `SABI_CALLER_ID=+2342017001459`. Trunk plumbing sound — endpoint `africastalking`, identify match `102.223.37.68`, `context=from-at`, alaw; ufw allows 5060/udp and RTP 10000-10100 from that IP; zero auth failures. Still unproven: audio over AT. Only AT outbound ever placed at 18:14:59 → `Called +18604367048@africastalking` → ringing → progress → never answered.

**Side defect**: voice box has **no `AT_API_KEY` at all**, so `emergency_alert._send_at_sms()` returns "not configured" and safeguarding SMS falls through to Twilio trial — can only ever reach Naomi's own verified US number.

### August 21 — first-ever answered AT call, but heard silence (session de04c6a7)
`+2348124724459` flashed the line, answered the callback. First AT leg ever to reach `answered` — proof of trunk→flash→callback→AudioSocket chain end to end on AT. `AS_UUID=fc0727b8…`, new Supabase student row `e2dec2c7-f4e9-401f-979f-2eb2cbdb871b`, realtime session started (not Gemini Live — she's not on `SABI_GEMINI_LIVE_PHONES`, landed in older `voice_realtime.py` lane).

**Then it broke on TTS, not telephony**: `ElevenLabs TTS failed: 402 Payment Required` (lapsed account) → fell back to YarnGPT → `YarnGPT TTS failed: The read operation timed out` → `RuntimeError: All TTS providers failed`. She got 8 seconds of dead air, AudioSocket errored, Asterisk hung up. `duration=16 billsec=9 user_turns=0 assistant_turns=1`. Two minutes later system auto-retried second callback (post-hangup trigger) — she didn't answer.

So audio over AT is **proven to connect. Not proven to work.** Blocker is `SABI_TTS_PRIMARY=elevenlabs` on dead account — first time it cost an actual Nigerian caller a real (bad) experience. Fixing TTS fallback (or flipping primary off ElevenLabs) is now single highest-priority item before any tester round.

### August 22 — website demo debugged
Website demo (`sabi.eduforequality.org/demo` → `/sabi-voice`) was broken Aug 22: `/api/sabi` called Claude only, Anthropic out of credits, and Llama 3.3 IDs 404'd on Groq/Cerebras. Live path now `callSabiVoiceFast` (Cerebras `gpt-oss-120b` → Groq `openai/gpt-oss-120b` → Claude). Cerebras production key currently 402 unpaid; Groq is what actually greets. First-name turns wrongly treated as PII → now pass. Returning-user "Continue with Sabi" still 500'd because Groq `openai/gpt-oss-120b` spends `max_tokens` on hidden reasoning; at 200 tokens `finish_reason=length`, `content` empty, unpaid Claude 500s. Fix: `max_tokens` 512, empty-content fallback to `openai/gpt-oss-20b`, 429 retry, skip broken Vercel RAG embed, don't 500 on dead Claude. Driven locally: "Hello Naomi! Do you go to school?" Website TTS: ElevenLabs Bukola library voice 402s on free plan; `/api/sabi/tts` fell to YarnGPT (~4.5s) then to browser `speechSynthesis`. Live chain now Chatterbox (Naomi at `api.eduforequality.org/afro-tts/tts`) → ElevenLabs → YarnGPT.

## 2. Every architectural / technical decision, laid out

**LLM chain evolution**:
- Claude Haiku primary throughout (currently `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001`), with Ollama Llama 3.1 8B self-hosted fallback (dating from spring)
- Cerebras Llama-3.3-70b was configured as "first fast path" ahead of Claude → **exposed dead** in June 22 red-team (Cerebras account had NO model access, both `llama3.1-8b` and `llama-3.3-70b` 404) → later Jul 7 verified `CEREBRAS_API_KEY` not in Hetzner `.env` at all, so dormant on prod (`LLM primary: Claude claude-haiku-4-5`). Live LLM order: Claude Haiku → Groq → Ollama.
- June 15: prompt tightened to ≤12 spoken words + one short question + max_tokens 90 across Claude/Cerebras/Groq/Ollama
- Web `callSabiVoiceFast` path: Cerebras `gpt-oss-120b` → Groq `openai/gpt-oss-120b` → Claude (Aug 22). Cerebras prod key 402 unpaid; Groq greets.
- August 7-8: on Naomi's number ONLY, replaced Claude+Groq+STT chain with a single continuous `gemini-3.1-flash-live-preview` Live session (voice `Kore` → briefly `Leda` → back to `Kore`; `SABI_GEMINI_LIVE_VOICE=auto` sends no voice).

**STT chain evolution**:
- Groq Whisper large-v3 primary (fixed conf 0.82) → local faster-whisper fallback; literacy uses separate prompt + local-salvage when Groq returns suspect words

## Cross-cutting themes worth noting

**Naomi is the only technical builder.** Every summer sprint sits on top of running code she can't easily hand off. The pilot documentation lives in no git repo. Multiple times a "fix" was reverted by an auto-save commit or was never actually deployed despite being committed.

**The single hardest technical problem is 8 kHz noisy Nigerian children speaking numbers.** Every off-the-shelf model fails; Whisper hits 124% WER on Nigerian film audio; Awarri Whisper-Small hallucinates fluent adult sentences on noisy child turns. The moat, if there is one, is per-child identity on a shared phone (nobody else solves it) + collecting a Nigerian children's noisy telephony data corpus with training consent + eventually fine-tuning a local Whisper.

**The Gemini Live pivot is real but incomplete.** It solves the child-hears-Sabi latency problem (one continuous session, native barge-in), gives up literacy and provider fallback, and — critically — loses the deterministic child-safety layer. As of Aug 20, only Naomi's number is on it; commit `6cbe0ea` to promote it to production is landed but not deployed.

**Key files/paths for the next Claude**:
- Server code: `/opt/sabi/sabi-server/` (production, files scp'd in), git checkout stale at commit `3b51788`
- Prompt: `sabi-server/prompts/sabi_tutor_prompt.md` (Live lane) + `original_sabi_prompt.py` + inline `_FALLBACK_TUTOR_PROMPT` in `gemini_live.py`
- Guardrails: `curriculum-app/lib/voice/guardrails.ts` + `sabi-server/guardrails.py`

Here's the honest state:

**Yes, literacy is currently down for phone calls — hard-blocked in two independent places:**

1. **Every Gemini Live call is force-injected with**: *"The caller is currently in a NUMERACY-ONLY test. Do not teach literacy, letter sounds, spelling, or phonics during this call."* This is unconditional — it fires for every caller, not just yours. It's on line 1180 of `gemini_live.py`.
2. **The tutor prompt file** (`sabi_tutor_prompt.md`) — 100% naira-market math, zero phonics/reading content. Even if you removed the numeracy-only injection, the prompt has no literacy material to lean on.

So: today's Gemini Live Sabi *cannot* teach literacy for a demo, and this isn't an env-var flip — the code deliberately blocks it and the prompt doesn't cover it.

**How real would it be to add for your dad's demo?** Two paths, very different effort levels:

1. **Real fix (right way, hours of work)**: Add a literacy section to `sabi_tutor_prompt.md`, remove the numeracy-only injection when the learner's state is literacy, port the literacy diagnostic logic from `voice_realtime.py` into the Gemini Live path, test it. Real work, not "flip a switch."

2. **Fast demo hack (30 minutes, honest about tradeoffs)**:
   - Add a small `SABI_LITERACY_MODE_PHONES=+18604367048` env override that swaps the numeracy-only injection for a literacy-only injection

## Claude · 2026-09-06T20:41:45.686Z

**Found it — two real problems, one is almost certainly your issue right now:**

**Problem 1 (likely THE cause of your "could not start call"):** Vercel has `SABI_SERVER_URL` set (17 days ago = pre-outage, pointing at the old Hetzner IP or DNS). But the Vercel API code reads `SABI_BACKEND_URL` first, then `NEXT_PUBLIC_SABI_BACKEND_URL`, then falls back to `api.eduforequality.org`. It **doesn't read `SABI_SERVER_URL`** at all — despite that being the one that's set. So the fallback fires — `api.eduforequality.org`. That DNS-resolves to `139.84.246.103` (I checked earlier), so it *should* work — but the code might be hitting an issue with the actual request format.

Let me trace this more carefully — it might not be a URL problem, it might be the FormData body Vercel sends failing when the sabi-server expects form-urlencoded. Actually I hit it directly and it worked using `-F` (multipart form data), which is what `new FormData()` produces in Node — so that should be fine.

**Problem 2 (real, less likely the immediate cause):** the board member allowlist. Your email in the E4E context is `naomi.ivie04@gmail.com` and `naomi.u.ivie@tuck.dartmouth.edu` — both in the list. But if you happen to be signed in on some other account (Wesleyan, personal, another test account), you'd get **403 "Advisor-board access required"**, not "could not start call."

**Since you clicked a real button and got "could not start call"** — that's likely a 500 error from the API, not 401/403 (those would show different messages). So it's more likely a config issue than an auth one.

Two things to figure out which it is — can you tell me:

1. **Which email are you signed in as on `eduforequality.org` right now?** (top-right of the page)
2. **Open the browser DevTools console** (Right-click → Inspect → Console tab) and try clicking "Call" again — you'll see the exact HTTP status code and error message. That single line will tell me whether it's auth, config, or something in the proxy call.

Alternatively, since I just verified the sabi-server side is 100% working via direct curl, **your test call from a moment ago is on its way to your phone right now** — the direct-curl one I sent as part of the diagnosis. If that phone rings in the next 10 seconds, that also confirms it's a Vercel-side issue, not a phone/AT-side one.

## Claude · 2026-09-06T20:42:23.856Z

Just found the real cause — and I flagged this at 2:35pm and never went back to fix it: **the new Vultr box has no HTTPS certificate.**

Vercel's server-side fetch goes to `https://api.eduforequality.org/admin/asterisk/direct-call` (the code default because `SABI_SERVER_URL` is empty). The domain DNS-resolves correctly to the new box — but the new box has never been issued a Let's Encrypt cert. So Vercel's fetch fails on SSL handshake, throws an exception, and the API route surfaces it as "could not start call."

## Claude · 2026-09-06T20:56:56.237Z

Yes — a proper end-of-call IVR ("after the tone, please leave feedback for Sabi" → beep → record) is absolutely possible. That exact pattern is a standard Asterisk primitive.

Two honest things to know though before I say "and it's already there":

**1. Something very close already exists on the Live lane, but it's not exactly IVR-shaped.**
Per the code I just checked, when the call reaches its natural end, Sabi *speaks* an invitation ("before you go, is there anything you want to tell me about our call today?") and then the system opens a beep + record window (`FEEDBACK_OPEN_CUE`, `_capture_feedback_turn`). That's functionally an IVR — a prompt, a beep, a recording — just delivered by Sabi's voice instead of a synthetic IVR voice. From the caller's perspective it feels like a conversation ending softly rather than a robotic "PRESS 1 TO LEAVE A MESSAGE" — which is by design, per your product intent (`SABI_FEEDBACK_MODE=testers`, "open feedback space, not structured survey").

**2. If what you want is the classic robot-IVR feel** — hard tone, "at the tone, please leave your feedback... beep... [record]" like a legacy voicemail box — that's a slightly different implementation and yes, doable. Real Asterisk primitives to build it with, in about 30 minutes of work:
- `Playback(vm-intro)` or a pre-recorded MP3 with your exact wording
- `Beep()`
- `Record(...)` with a silence-timeout / max-duration
- Save the file, POST metadata to `/admin/feedback` so it lands in the same place as spoken feedback
- Optionally: DTMF `1` to save / `2` to re-record / `#` to end early

**Which flavor do you actually want?**
1. **"Sabi asks warmly, then records"** — already exists, just needs to be reconfirmed working end-to-end on a real call
2. **"After the tone, please leave feedback... BEEP"** — build the classic IVR pattern in Asterisk dialplan + a small FastAPI endpoint to accept the WAV

Also want to flag: **the deploy from a minute ago should be done now** — worth a hard-refresh (Cmd+Shift+R) on `/sabi/testers` to check if the testing-week text is gone. If it is, we know Vercel deploy is fine and we can move to the IVR without leaving a broken thread.

## Claude · 2026-09-06T21:21:30.549Z

Good — I'll answer the length question and keep building.

**What controls call length today**, from the code I've been in:

Three separate limits, in order of who wins:

1. **Hard deadline: `SABI_MAX_CALL_SECONDS=480`** (8 minutes) — asyncio kills the Gemini session at this timer regardless of state. Set in the container `.env`.
2. **Minimum lesson: `SABI_MIN_LESSON_SECONDS=300`** (5 minutes) — Sabi is *prevented* from wrapping up before this. If Gemini emits "goodbye" text before 300s, an early-wrap guard blocks it and re-injects a "keep teaching" instruction.
3. **Target wrap: `SABI_TARGET_WRAP_SECONDS=420`** (7 minutes) — Sabi is *encouraged* to start wrapping around here.

Plus the tutor prompt itself says *"target is 5 to 7 minutes"* right in the text.

**So the "5-7 minute" range you're hearing is coming from all three of those working together.** To make the literacy lesson meaningfully longer, real options:

- **Simple bump for tonight**: raise `SABI_MAX_CALL_SECONDS` (say 600 = 10 min), `SABI_MIN_LESSON_SECONDS` (say 420 = 7 min), `SABI_TARGET_WRAP_SECONDS` (say 540 = 9 min), and edit the "target 5 to 7 minutes" line in the literacy prompt to say "target 7 to 10 minutes". Whole-lane change.
- **Or**: make lesson length lane-specific (numeracy stays 5-7 min, literacy runs 7-10 min). Slightly more code, more control.

Which one do you want, and roughly how long should the literacy lesson be — 7-10 min? 10-12 min? Longer? I'll pick that + the day rotation together in one deploy.

Real root cause found: **the early-wrap guard is gated on `GEMINI_LIVE_TOOLS_ENABLED`**, and tools are `off` (your Aug-20 choice). So the guard is completely disabled site-wide — for both numeracy and literacy — not just for the literacy lane. Numeracy calls happen to run 5-6 min naturally, so nobody noticed; literacy is shorter per exchange so Sabi wraps at 2 min with no guard to stop her.

Applying three fixes in one deploy:

1. **Enable the early-wrap guard even when tools=off** — the `is_early_wrap_text()` check is a pure text/regex scan, doesn't need tools. Remove the `GEMINI_LIVE_TOOLS_ENABLED and` gate.
2. **Make the enforcement text lane-aware** (currently it says "call get_next_numeracy_problem" — wrong on literacy).
3. **Bump literacy lesson length to 8-10 minutes** (min 480s, target 600s, max 720s) so Sabi has time to walk through the phonics sequence properly.

## Claude · 2026-09-06T21:46:22.075Z

Enough real data pulled — I don't need to keep guessing at column names. Here's the honest read:

**Direct answer: NO, she's not really advancing between calls right now — and the state proves it.**

Naomi's stored literacy state in Supabase (from your actual saved profile):

```
literacy.current_module: 1
literacy.current_lesson: 1
literacy.current_week: 1
literacy.active_skill: phonemic_awareness_beginning
literacy.diagnostic_status: done
literacy.diagnostic_results.status: placed
literacy.diagnostic_results.failed_item: "Let's play a sound game. What sound do you hear at the very beginning of the word ball?"
literacy.next_step: "Continue the exact current literacy lesson with one guided example and one independent check."
```

**What this means:**
- The **numeracy diagnostic ran once way back** (that's what dropped you at Module 4, Week 13, Lesson 3 in numeracy — real advancement history from prior calls).
- The **literacy diagnostic ran once** and placed you at Module 1 / Week 1 / Lesson 1 (beginning). Since then, **it hasn't moved.** Same module, same lesson, every call.
- Sabi's Gemini Live literacy lane **does read the state at the start of each call** (that's how she knows to teach Phase 1 Module 1 stuff), but she doesn't **advance** the state at the end. There's no code path that says "child got 8 questions right → bump lesson from 1 to 2."

**Why**: the mastery/advancement machinery you built (curriculum_mastery.py, mastery_probe.py, layer B/C evidence) only runs on the **turn-based** lane, not on Gemini Live. On the Live lane, the tools were the mechanism that graded/advanced — and you have tools OFF right now (Aug 20 preference). Without tools, there's no grader, so no advancement gets written back to `learning_state`.

**So functionally today**: every literacy call is a "Day 1 of Module 1, Lesson 1" replay. She might vary the exact activity within that lesson (which is what you heard in the two calls), but you'd never see her advance to Module 2 or Week 2 no matter how many times you call.

**You said don't change anything — so I'm not.** Just naming it clearly so you know the current state:
- ✅ Literacy calls work, feel good, sound right
- ❌ No between-call progression tracking on the Gemini Live lane
- ⚠️ Real fix (whenever you want) = wire an end-of-call heuristic that advances `learning_state.literacy.current_lesson` if X of Y turns were correct, similar to how the turn-based lane already does it

**Direct answer: the website is partially broken.** Same problem I flagged earlier today at ~4pm and it still hasn't been fixed:

| Route | Status | What it means |
|---|---|---|
| `/sabi-voice` page | ✅ 200 | The landing page loads fine — visitor sees a working site |
| `/api/sabi` text chat | ✅ 200 | Text pipeline is healthy — Sabi replies with real text: *"Hello! I'm Sabi, your learning buddy…"* |
| **`/api/sabi/tts` voice synth** | ❌ **500** — `"TTS generation failed"` | Almost certainly ElevenLabs 402 (paid voice, free account) → Chatterbox (unreachable from Vercel because api.eduforequality.org is now the Vultr box, no GPU) → YarnGPT (timeout) → 500 |
| **`/api/sabi/voice-chat` full mic loop** | ❌ **500** — `"Voice chat pipeline failed"` | Depends on TTS working, so it inherits the same failure |

**So a visitor who clicks "Talk to Sabi" on the site gets the mic button, speaks, and gets an error.** Same bad-optics problem you flagged earlier — "our stuff not working in demos."

**Root cause:** ElevenLabs free-tier can't produce Bukola-voice audio (the voice hardcoded in the TTS chain). The paid Chatterbox alternative that used to run on the Hetzner GPU box isn't there anymore (we moved to Vultr, which is CPU-only — no GPU, no Chatterbox). So both preferred TTS paths are dead, and YarnGPT (the last-resort fallback) is either slow or unreachable from Vercel.

**Two real ways to fix this today:**
1. **Switch the website's default voice from Bukola to a free ElevenLabs voice** (Sarah, Bella, etc. — I just verified they return 200 on this same key). Fast: config change on Vercel or in the Vercel-hosted route. Sounds different from Bukola but it works.
2. **Pay ElevenLabs** — cheapest plan is $5/mo Starter, unlocks Bukola and stops today's outage in 5 minutes. Real recurring cost.

Which do you want to do?

## Claude · 2026-09-22T03:57:25.599Z

That's not the API key — it's the **key ID** (an internal identifier), not the actual usable key. ElevenLabs is explicit in the error:

> "API key ID used as API key — only valid API keys can be used. API keys start with 'sk_' and are shown when the key is created or rotated."

**Real ElevenLabs API keys start with `sk_`** and are only shown once, at the moment you create or rotate the key. The `ade529dc4c619a...` string you sent is what appears alongside the key in the dashboard as its ID, but it doesn't authenticate.

**How to get the actual key:**
1. Go to `elevenlabs.io/app/settings/api-keys`
2. Find the key you meant (or click **Create API Key** for a new one)
3. Copy the value that **starts with `sk_`** — it's only revealed at creation time
4. Paste that here and I'll wire it in

**Note this doesn't fix the paid-voice problem on its own** — the current live key on Vercel (`sk_27...`, 206 days old) authenticates fine, but hits 402 on Bukola because the account is free-tier. A new key from the same account will do the same thing. Two things get us actually working:
- New `sk_...` key **AND** the underlying account has enough credit for the Bukola voice, OR
- Switch the website to a free-tier voice (Sarah, Bella, etc.) with the current key

Which are we solving for?
